  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Montezuma, leader of The Aztecs (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Aztecs
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Jaguar, Replacing Longswordsman (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Unique Unit: Otomi, Replacing Warrior (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Telpochcalli, Replacing Library (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Huey Teocalli, Replacing Grand Temple (Requires technology: Theology)
        - Civilization Ability: Killing enemy Units grants 150% of their Strength as Gold and Faith. When you win a war (War Score of 25+), a Golden Age begins.
      - Leader: Montezuma
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Lasers: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Particle Physics, Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      
      ## Policies
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Rationalism (New Branch):
        - Rationalism focuses on maximizing Growth and Science output.
        - Adopting Rationalism grants:
        - * +2 Production and +3 Science from all Strategic Resources.
        - * +5% Science in all Cities.
        - Each Rationalism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +2% Science.
        - Adopting all Policies in Rationalism grants:
        - * Unlocks building Bletchley Park.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +12 City Security in every City.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Scientists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Statecraft (New Branch):
        - Statecraft improves your ability to benefit from Trade Routes, City-States, Spies, and the World Congress.
        - Adopting Statecraft grants:
        - * +1 of every Yield in Capital for every 20 Citizens in your Empire.
        - * +1 Gold in every City.
        - Each Statecraft policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Gold in every City, and +10% Influence from Diplomatic Missions.
        - Adopting all policies in Statecraft grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Palace of Westminster.
        - * Every World Congress Session, gain 5 Culture, Science, and Gold for each Delegate you control, scaling with Era.
        - * World Wonders require 1 less unlocked Policy for every 3 City-State alliances you maintain.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Diplomats with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 9
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 5
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 3
        - Boldness: 10

        - HostileBias: 9

        - DenounceWillingness: 8

        - Chattiness: 3
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - Rationale: Maximum aggression profile maintained. Co-Prosperity Sphere enables bullying Persian city-states (Zurich, Warsaw, Trowulan) to strip 14 delegates before World Congress. MinorCivWarBias 10 critical for this strategy. Boldness 10 commits all forces to Moson Kahni capture before Brazil steals it. Loyalty 2 preserved for strategic flexibility regarding vassals post-capitulation.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Lasers
        - Rationale: Select Lasers as next technology to unlock Nuclear Missiles. Denmark has completed Manhattan Project and could deploy nuclear weapons imminently - need deterrent capability before Copenhagen final assault. Brazil has Apollo Program and represents existential Science Victory threat - nuclear missiles essential for both offensive city-breaking megatonnage and strategic deterrence once their spaceship parts begin production.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Lightning Warfare (Policy)
        - Rationale: Maintain Lightning Warfare as selected next policy (available in 11 turns). This Level 2 Autocracy tenet provides +3 Great General movement, +15% attack and ZOC-ignoring for Gunpowder units, and +15% attack with +1 Movement for Armored units. Critical for upcoming Brazil campaign where speed across large territory is essential given their 15,788 military strength and potential to complete Science Victory.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Argos
        - Public: -10
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: Argos joined our war against Siam this turn alongside Winchester, showing cooperation despite previous betrayal when they flipped to Siam. Improving relationship from -20/-40 to -10/-30 acknowledges this military coordination while maintaining appropriate caution. They remain a guarded partner rather than trusted ally, but current shared enemy justifies slight warming.
        - UpdatedTurn: 128
        
        ### Siam
        - Public: -25
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: Siam provides 22 GPT and trade routes as our vassal. With Martial Law policy, the garrison benefits make maintaining vassal status profitable. Moderate hostility maintained in case we need to seize Muang Saluang (with School of Philosophy and 10 wonders) if their loyalty wavers or when vassalage breaks. Current arrangement is economically beneficial.
        - UpdatedTurn: 309
        
        ### The Maya
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Maya have 16 wonders in Palenque (Great Lighthouse, Hanging Gardens, Porcelain Tower, Statue of Liberty, etc.) essential for domination victory score. Maximum hostility maintained. War score at 74%, continue siege of Palenque, Tikal, Chichen Itza.
        - UpdatedTurn: 355
        
        ### America
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: America fully defeated and vassalized. Peace treaty signed this turn with capitulation. Maximum hostility maintained to prevent any resistance or diplomatic maneuvering. Their remaining cities (New York, Boston, etc.) will be fully integrated.
        - UpdatedTurn: 355
        
        ### Denmark
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Denmark completed Manhattan Project - existential threat requiring immediate Copenhagen capture. Maximum hostility maintained. Their 2,265 military vs my 36,854 is trivial, but nuclear weapons capability makes speed essential. Defensive pact with Persia complicates strategy - both must be defeated. Copenhagen contains Pyramids, Colossus, and TwoKay Foods HQ making it extremely high-value. Full assault authorized, no peace until capitulation.
        - UpdatedTurn: 413
        
        ### Persia
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Maximum hostility toward Persia continues. Co-Prosperity Sphere successfully triggered - city-states Zurich, Warsaw, Trowulan, and Abernethy (all Persian allies) declared war when I allied with Cape Town, stripping Persia of 14 delegates before World Congress in 4 turns. War score only 2% with progress stalled - need to accelerate Persepolis invasion while their city-state allies are distracted. Persepolis contains Heroic Epic and The Motherland Calls wonder.
        - UpdatedTurn: 413
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Brazil remains existential ideological and victory threat. Freedom vs Autocracy (-999) guarantees permanent war. They have 14 delegates, 4 civs already influenced culturally, and Apollo Program completed. Positioned Special Forces near former Shoshone territory (Te-Moak). Will deal with Brazil after securing Copenhagen and Persepolis - maintain maximum hostility to prevent diplomatic normalization.
        - UpdatedTurn: 413
        
        ### The Shoshone
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Shoshone new vassal with shared Autocracy ideology (+999). Moson Kahni capitulated successfully. They provide 31 GPT tribute and military coordination. Shared ideology and vassalage creates permanent alliance. Maximum friendliness supports cooperative use of their territory for staging against Persia/Denmark and trade route benefits.
        - UpdatedTurn: 413
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Maintain existing maximum hostility relationships with Denmark (-100/-100), Persia (-100/-100), and Brazil (-100/-100). All three represent existential threats - Denmark with nukes, Persia with city-state delegates (15), Brazil with Apollo Program. No diplomatic normalization possible. Co-Prosperity Sphere successfully stripped Persia of 14 delegates before World Congress (6 turns). Maximum aggression persona (Boldness 10, WarBias 10, MinorCivWarBias 10) maintained for domination push.
        - GrandStrategy: Conquest

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 41
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 5 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Ban Luxury: Crab:
            - Description: A Luxury resource is designated as immoral, endangered, or otherwise inappropriate for use by the general population. As long as the ban is in place, Civilizations receive no Happiness or Monopoly benefits from this Luxury resource.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Global Peace Accords:
            - Description: Unit maintenance costs are raised by 25% Gold. 2.0x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 0.5x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: 417
          - Persian Sphere of Influence: Trowulan:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Persian Sphere of Influence: Warsaw:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Persian Sphere of Influence: Zurich:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Persian Sphere of Influence: Abernethy:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 417
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: 395
          - World Religion: Protestantism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - Proposer: The Shoshone
          - Enact: International Games:
            - Description: Begins the International Games project. Once underway, Civilizations can contribute Production towards its completion by selecting it in the city production list. When complete, Civilizations receive bonuses based on how much they contributed.
              : Olympic Village wonder appears in Capital. (Receive +3 Happiness and +3 Culture in the City in which it is built. 25% of the Culture from World Wonders and Tiles is added to the Tourism output of the City. All Stadiums receive +2 Gold and Tourism.)
              : Receive a free Great Person of your choice.
              : Tourism increases by 50% for 20 Turns.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Radio.
            - Proposer: Brazil
          - Enact: World Ideology: Autocracy:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - Proposer: Persia

        You, Montezuma (leader of The Aztecs, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 423.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Montezuma, leader of The Aztecs (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Aztecs
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Jaguar, Replacing Longswordsman (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Unique Unit: Otomi, Replacing Warrior (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Telpochcalli, Replacing Library (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Huey Teocalli, Replacing Grand Temple (Requires technology: Theology)
        - Civilization Ability: Killing enemy Units grants 150% of their Strength as Gold and Faith. When you win a war (War Score of 25+), a Golden Age begins.
      - Leader: Montezuma
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Lasers: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Particle Physics, Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      
      ## Policies
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Rationalism (New Branch):
        - Rationalism focuses on maximizing Growth and Science output.
        - Adopting Rationalism grants:
        - * +2 Production and +3 Science from all Strategic Resources.
        - * +5% Science in all Cities.
        - Each Rationalism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +2% Science.
        - Adopting all Policies in Rationalism grants:
        - * Unlocks building Bletchley Park.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +12 City Security in every City.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Scientists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Statecraft (New Branch):
        - Statecraft improves your ability to benefit from Trade Routes, City-States, Spies, and the World Congress.
        - Adopting Statecraft grants:
        - * +1 of every Yield in Capital for every 20 Citizens in your Empire.
        - * +1 Gold in every City.
        - Each Statecraft policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Gold in every City, and +10% Influence from Diplomatic Missions.
        - Adopting all policies in Statecraft grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Palace of Westminster.
        - * Every World Congress Session, gain 5 Culture, Science, and Gold for each Delegate you control, scaling with Era.
        - * World Wonders require 1 less unlocked Policy for every 3 City-State alliances you maintain.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Diplomats with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Lasers
        - Rationale: Maintain Lasers as the next technology to unlock Nuclear Missiles. Denmark has completed the Manhattan Project, and Brazil represents a severe victory threat. We need nuclear megatonnage to crack Brazil's high-defense cities (Rio de Janeiro at 118, Sao Paulo at 104) and establish a strategic nuclear deterrent.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: Maintain Lightning Warfare as the next policy selection. This Level 2 Autocracy tenet provides +3 Great General movement, +15% attack and ZOC-ignoring for gunpowder units, and +15% attack with +1 movement for armored units. This is critical for blitzing our Modern Armor through the vast territories of Brazil, Persia, and Denmark.
        - Policy: Lightning Warfare
    - #2:
      - toolName: keep-status-quo
      - rationale: We are maintaining our aggressive Conquest grand strategy and high-mobilization flavors to execute a final, decisive global campaign. We control 5 out of 8 original capitals and need only Copenhagen, Persepolis, and Rio de Janeiro to win a Domination Victory. 
        
        Existential Threat: Brazil is 5 to 14 turns away from winning a Cultural Victory. We must immediately launch a preemptive war against Brazil to sever their trade routes, break their open borders, and dismantle their cultural infrastructure. Brazil's Defensive Pact with Denmark will pull Copenhagen into the war, which perfectly aligns with our need to capture the Danish capital.
        
        Military Execution Plan:
        1. Air Campaign: Leverage our western airbases in Goshute and Bunkeya to launch simultaneous air strikes against Copenhagen (13-15 tiles away) and Persepolis (20 tiles away) using our Stealth and Heavy Bombers.
        2. Northern Brazil Offensive: Capture Te-Moak and Curitiba using local forces and aircraft to establish forward airbases to bomb Rio de Janeiro.
        3. Land Steamroll: Deploy our massive eastern army (Modern Armor, Mechanized Infantry, Rocket Artillery) westward to overwhelm the remnants of the Persian, Danish, and Brazilian defenses.
        
        World Congress Strategy: In 5 turns, our coalition (Aztecs + vassals) will control 46 out of 78 delegates (59%). We will vote to enact 'World Ideology: Autocracy' and 'Cold War', while decisively defeating Brazil's 'International Worlds' project to permanently crush their cultural victory chances.
        
        Economic Viability: Our negative GPT is irrelevant. The Aztec UA (150% Gold/Faith from kills) will fully fund this global war through unit destruction. No peace until total domination.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Expanding Borders Through Conquest**
            
            I'm currently focused on expanding our empire. We've secured five capitals so far: Sukhothai, Tenochtitlan, Moson Kahni, Washington, and Palenque. Our score stands at 556, reflecting our strong progress toward domination.
            
            **Evaluating Autocratic Ideologies**
            
            I'm currently weighing my next ideological policy. While Lightning Warfare is available and aligns with our aggressive expansion, I'm reviewing other Level 2 Autocracy tenets like Syndicalism and Mare Nostrum, alongside newly unlocked branches, to ensure the most strategic advantage.
            
            **Researching Future Advancements**
            
            My focus has shifted to future technologies, specifically Lasers and Telecommunications, though I notice our current research is Satellites with six turns remaining. This follows my previous selection of Lasers. I'm also reassessing our ongoing conflict with Persia and Denmark, considering their military strengths and alliances, even with Brazil's overwhelming power.
            
            **Confirming Vassal Status**
            
            I'm double-checking our controlled capitals and vassal relationships. It appears I previously miscounted, thinking we controlled Tenochtitlan as a capitulated capital; instead, it's a direct conquest. I'm now verifying ownership for Sukhothai (Siam), Washington (America), Moson Kahni (Shoshone), and Palenque (Maya), all of whom have capitulated.
            
            **Securing Remaining Capitals**
            
            I'm now zeroing in on the final capitals needed for victory. Copenhagen, Persepolis, and Rio de Janeiro are our remaining targets. I've discovered we're already at war with Persia, but need to confirm our status with Denmark to plan the final push.
            
            **Investigating Diplomatic Anomalies**
            
            I'm currently puzzled by our war with Persia and Denmark's non-involvement, despite their defensive pact. I'm reviewing the timeline of the pact and the war's initiation to understand this diplomatic anomaly before strategizing our next move against remaining capitals.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Pacts**
            
            I'm scrutinizing the intricate web of defensive pacts. Denmark's alliances with both Persia and Brazil are particularly intriguing, especially given our ongoing war with Persia. I'm dissecting the timing of these pacts relative to declarations of war to understand Denmark's non-involvement.
            
            **Assessing Global Conflicts**
            
            I'm analyzing the shifting alliances and war declarations. Sokoto's declaration of war on Persia, initiated by our alliance, has triggered counter-declarations from Persian allies against Sokoto. This complicates the Persia-Denmark situation. I'm also noting our negative gold per turn despite strong science and culture.
            
            **Strategizing Diplomatic Engagements**
            
            I'm focusing on our diplomatic efforts, particularly with Agent Chimalli rigging elections in Cape Town and Agent Cuicatl in Sokoto. It's concerning that we have several unassigned Vassal Diplomats; I need to deploy them strategically. My attention is also drawn to Brazil's strong lead in the Science Victory race.
            
            **Re-evaluating Military Strength**
            
            I'm assessing the military presence in key zones, noting the disparity between our considerable strength and the limited Persian forces in Pasargadae. The composition of units in Neutral Land Zone 9260, including Shoshone, Persian, and Aztec forces, requires careful consideration for potential engagements.
            
            **Confirming Alliance Dynamics**
            
            I'm verifying the recent shift in Sokoto's allegiance from Brazil to us, which directly precipitated their war with Persia. This is a key development, as it explains why Persian allies are now warring with Sokoto, even though Denmark has remained uninvolved despite their pact with Persia.
            
            **Preventing Cultural Domination**
            
            I'm now deeply concerned about Brazil's impending Cultural Victory. They are rapidly gaining influence over me, the Shoshone, and the Maya. I must immediately assess if declaring war on them will disrupt their tourism and prevent their win before they complete the Citizen Earth Protocol.
            
            **Disrupting Brazilian Tourism**
            
            I'm now focused on disrupting Brazil's imminent Cultural Victory. I need to carefully consider our active trade deals with them before considering a declaration of war to halt their tourism and prevent their win.
            
            **Locating Scattered Forces**
            
            I'm reassessing our troop positions after realizing my core forces are far from the Persian front. My previous analysis focused on Shoshone territory, but our military strength is concentrated in our eastern core, with only limited units present in neutral or contested zones.
            
            **Positioning Western Forces**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our troop deployment, realizing my primary army is concentrated in the east, not positioned to engage Persia or Brazil from our western cities. My western territories, while boasting significant air power, lack sufficient land units for a strong offensive push.
            
            **Repositioning Eastern Armies**
            
            My core fighting strength is surprisingly distant from the Persian front. I'm now reassessing the tactical AI's unit movement, aiming to shift our primary army from the east to western territories, enabling a more direct confrontation with Persia and Brazil.
            
            **Disrupting Brazilian Cultural Victory**
            
            I'm now frantically strategizing to halt Brazil's impending Cultural Victory. Their influence is rapidly spreading, and they're on the cusp of securing dominance over seven civilizations. I must disrupt their tourism immediately by canceling open borders and considering a direct war.
            
            **Preparing for Decisive Conflict**
            
            I'm now intensely focused on countering Brazil's imminent cultural win. Open borders and trade routes with them, even through vassals, are being severed. The key is to disrupt their tourism, potentially by capturing their wonder-laden cities. Denmark's defensive pact with Brazil presents an opportunity, as their low military strength makes them an accessible target for us anyway.
            
            **Initiating Total War**
            
            I've confirmed we possess the Uranium needed for nuclear capabilities and have strategically positioned our nuclear power plants. With Brazil's imminent cultural victory, I've realized declaring war on them will automatically engage Denmark due to their pact, and we are already at war with Persia. This allows for a simultaneous, decisive push for domination against all remaining independent powers, leveraging our substantial military advantage.
            
            **Launching Global War**
            
            My focus has shifted to a decisive, all-out global war. With overwhelming military superiority over Denmark and ongoing conflict with Persia, declaring war on Brazil will engage them and potentially others, allowing me to secure domination and prevent their cultural win.
            
            **Verifying Relationship Parameters**
            
            I'm confirming that all major civilization relationships are appropriately set for our current global war. All opponents are at maximum hostility, and our vassals are at maximum friendliness. My persona traits are also aligned with our aggressive, conquest-focused strategy.
            
            **Researching Deterrent Capabilities**
            
            My focus is on unlocking nuclear capabilities. Lasers are now confirmed as the next research target to achieve this, vital for overcoming high-defense cities and deterring threats. I've also finalized the rationale for this choice, emphasizing its necessity against Brazil and Denmark.
            
            **Choosing Lightning Warfare**
            
            I've decided to maintain Lightning Warfare as my next policy. This choice significantly bolsters the combat and movement capabilities of my armored and gunpowder units, which is essential for rapid conquest against Brazil and Persia.
            
            **Confirming Conquest Flavors**
            
            I'm confirming that our current game flavors are perfectly aligned for a full-scale conquest. With our focus on military, mobilization, air supremacy, and happiness, and considering the imminent global war against Brazil, Denmark, and Persia, maintaining the status quo with these flavors is the optimal path. Our Aztec UA will ensure our campaigns fund themselves.
            
            **Securing Ideological Dominance**
            
            I'm focused on securing Autocracy as the World Ideology. This aligns perfectly with our faction and our vassals, hindering Brazil and Denmark. I'm also prioritizing opposing Brazil's International Worlds project, as their cultural victory is imminent.
            
            **Securing World Congress Control**
            
            I've confirmed absolute control of the World Congress with 59% of the delegates, ensuring the enactment of Autocracy as the World Ideology and the defeat of Brazil's International Worlds project. This effectively cripples their cultural victory path and solidifies my diplomatic dominance.
            
            **Positioning Air Power Effectively**
            
            I'm analyzing our bomber positions relative to key targets. Stealth and Heavy Bombers based at Goshute, Bunkeya, and Washakie can effectively strike both Persepolis and Copenhagen, proving an exceptional strategic advantage for our western air offensive.
            
            **Launching Multi-Pronged Assault**
            
            I'm now orchestrating a three-pronged offensive. An air campaign targets Copenhagen and Persepolis. Simultaneously, ground and air assaults aim to capture Te-Moak and Curitiba, establishing forward airbases to assault Rio de Janeiro.
            
            **Advancing Eastern Armies**
            
            My massive eastern army, composed of modern armor and mechanized infantry, is rapidly advancing towards Persia and Denmark, poised to steamroll their defenses. Our technologies and policies are already confirmed, and our focus is on executing this high-level military and diplomatic strategy.
            
            **Finalizing Preparations for Global Conflict**
            
            I've finalized my strategic decisions, confirming "Lasers" for research to unlock nuclear capabilities and "Lightning Warfare" as the next policy to boost armored units. My grand strategy and conquest flavors remain unchanged, perfectly aligned with the impending total war against Brazil, Denmark, and Persia, ensuring our ultimate domination.
            
            **Initiating Multi-Front Assault**
            
            I am now launching a three-pronged offensive. Air power will simultaneously strike Copenhagen and Persepolis. Concurrently, ground and air forces will capture Te-Moak and Curitiba, establishing forward airbases to assault Rio de Janeiro. My eastern armies are also advancing to overwhelm Persia and Denmark.

        - #2:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: fCfrsmPCDlOZTNLW
          - toolName: set-policy
          - input:
            - Rationale: Maintain Lightning Warfare as the next policy selection. This Level 2 Autocracy tenet provides +3 Great General movement, +15% attack and ZOC-ignoring for gunpowder units, and +15% attack with +1 movement for armored units. This is critical for blitzing our Modern Armor through the vast territories of Brazil, Persia, and Denmark.
            - Policy: Lightning Warfare
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #3:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: 8powdHIG0XmZsSmW
          - toolName: keep-status-quo
          - input:
            - Rationale: We are maintaining our aggressive Conquest grand strategy and high-mobilization flavors to execute a final, decisive global campaign. We control 5 out of 8 original capitals and need only Copenhagen, Persepolis, and Rio de Janeiro to win a Domination Victory. 
              
              Existential Threat: Brazil is 5 to 14 turns away from winning a Cultural Victory. We must immediately launch a preemptive war against Brazil to sever their trade routes, break their open borders, and dismantle their cultural infrastructure. Brazil's Defensive Pact with Denmark will pull Copenhagen into the war, which perfectly aligns with our need to capture the Danish capital.
              
              Military Execution Plan:
              1. Air Campaign: Leverage our western airbases in Goshute and Bunkeya to launch simultaneous air strikes against Copenhagen (13-15 tiles away) and Persepolis (20 tiles away) using our Stealth and Heavy Bombers.
              2. Northern Brazil Offensive: Capture Te-Moak and Curitiba using local forces and aircraft to establish forward airbases to bomb Rio de Janeiro.
              3. Land Steamroll: Deploy our massive eastern army (Modern Armor, Mechanized Infantry, Rocket Artillery) westward to overwhelm the remnants of the Persian, Danish, and Brazilian defenses.
              
              World Congress Strategy: In 5 turns, our coalition (Aztecs + vassals) will control 46 out of 78 delegates (59%). We will vote to enact 'World Ideology: Autocracy' and 'Cold War', while decisively defeating Brazil's 'International Worlds' project to permanently crush their cultural victory chances.
              
              Economic Viability: Our negative GPT is irrelevant. The Aztec UA (150% Gold/Faith from kills) will fully fund this global war through unit destruction. No peace until total domination.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
